### Page 1

20260612 艦長QA重點紀要
核心讀者：基金經理人、產業分析師 | 發布日期：2026年6月12日
文件概述： 本報告針對2026年6月12日舉行之大型演講問答（QA）環節進行深度內容摘要，旨在為機構投資人
（Portfolio  Managers）與科技產業分析師（Analysts）提供關鍵的供應鏈技術轉移、客戶動態及產能預估。
本次QA核心聚焦於大客戶（Google等）超大型架構與AI晶片封裝設計的變更、晶片外掛光學元件（OEO/
CPO）時程、各家ASIC與CPU晶片架構競爭、以及先進封裝（E-interconnect/CoWoS）的良率瓶頸與潛在商
機。
一、 AI架構與晶片外掛光學（CPO/OEO）時程
800G與超大型Scale-Out網路時程： 業內高度關注的NVL76/76與下一代超大集群架構。明（2027）年搭
載NVIDIA新平台的量產趨勢明確，且後（2028）年起將大幅擴展。
CPO / OEO（全光網路）切換節點： 市場傳言CPO可能於2029年導入。艦長指出，若2029年將OEO封裝直

接整合或大舉大宗導入GPU旁，確實符合全光網路發展進程。然而，短中期內（2028年左右），在
48V Ready的電力架構下，由於高速訊號傳輸無法再使用傳統的PCB板，因此訊號從處理器拉出後，將全
面走向光通訊或專屬高速連接結構。
Spectrum-6交換機與客戶動態： Broadcom（博通）主導的Spectrum-6交換器方案預計於明（2027）年
開始在大型雲端客戶（如Google）進行數萬台規模的初期量產與測試。
二、 大客戶ASIC與主要Foundry/Service供應商競爭格局
1. Google TPU B9 / V9 與 V10 / B10 晶片佈局
TPU  B9（現行架構）： 目前核心由Marvell（美滿電子）提供Service。市場傳言Google意圖提升聯發科
（MediaTek）的供貨比重以降低對博通（Broadcom）與美滿的依賴。艦長評論，將ASIC代工百分之百移
轉為極為劇烈的供應鏈結構轉變，背後通常牽涉到非技術性的特定商業考量，而非僅為單純的分散供應商風

險。
博通（Broadcom）的關鍵角色： 博通並非毫無反制產品。其目前研發的八通道處理器方案（Sunfish
Ultra），本質上是將兩顆晶片封裝在一起（業界稱為Whalefish）。目前該款晶片定位更偏向Anthropic等
特定大模型客戶。若Google自研的TPU由聯發科主導，則外賣形式的Rack方案則預計繼續交由博通處理
（因博通具備較完整的CPO、光通訊及網絡交換器Rack整合能力）。
TPU  V10  /  B10（下一代架構）： 供應鏈目前觀察到，V10世代方案代號為Icefish，目前全數由聯發科
（MediaTek）主導，此代產品線中暫未看到博通的產品身影。
博通的長線替代晶片（Mirage）： 博通另有一顆代號為Mirage的TPU-like產品線，預計於2029年量產。不
過此晶片目前的定位與市場接受度較為特殊，與Google的核心合作量能尚未放大，主要仍以Sunfish家族系
列的光通訊Rack整合為大宗。
2. 美滿電子（Marvell）與其他周邊晶片商角色

美滿電子（Marvell）在Google  TPU生態系中主要提供周邊搭配方案（如CXL  Controller等），並預計在
V10世代提供類似LPU（Language Processing Unit）的周邊晶片支持，而非主導TPU核心晶片。

### Page 2

三、 先進封裝良率瓶頸、技術變革（E-interconnect vs CoWoS）
【核心投資爭點】客戶是否會因封裝瓶頸離開台積電？
艦長給予明確且強烈的投資指引：絕對沒有任何主要客戶能真正離開台積電。 Google、博通等雲端或晶片大
廠並非傳統半導體大廠，對半導體先進製程與封裝技術缺乏長期的底層信賴與自主研發根基。非台積電陣營
（如Intel、Samsung）在先進製程與封裝的踩雷（技術故障）機率極高，大客戶在多次嘗試並遭遇挫折後，
最終仍會回歸台積電先進封裝生態系。博通之前曾計畫將晶片全數轉往Intel代工，但最終受限於技術成熟度，
仍須依賴台積電。
1. E-interconnect（Intel 方案）與良率挑戰
市場普遍關注大客戶下一代晶片因Die Size（晶粒尺寸）過大，導致傳統CoWoS封裝出現Warping（翹曲）
問題。現行嘗試的解決方案包括將Carrier更換為玻璃材質（Glass  Substrate），藉以提升平整度並降低翹
曲率。
Intel力推的E-interconnect封裝技術，雖然技術概念先進，但其最大的技術難點在於Mix-Bump（混合凸

塊）製程。由於主晶片通常需要融合兩種不同Foundry（代工廠）的製程技術，必須同時在基板上處理
Micro-Bump（微凸塊）與Standard-Bump（標準凸塊）。此類複雜凸塊製程在應力與焊接良率上面臨極
大挑戰，目前仍处于研發與小規模測試階段。
2. 產能與良率的連鎖效應（對NVIDIA之利弊）
若大客戶的晶片因先進封裝良率無法突破而導致出貨延遲，這對NVIDIA（輝達）而言將是最大的利多。
由於NVIDIA目前在整個供應鏈中具備極強的產能綁定與控制力（包括在CoWoS產能、先進散熱、高頻寬記
憶體HBM等周邊料件上皆已與一線供應商提早簽訂排他或優先供應協議），若美系雲端大廠自研ASIC受
阻，市場AI算力需求將全數倒向NVIDIA方案。
四、 CPU市場發展與CSP自主架構趨勢
ARM 架構全面超越 X86 趨勢明確： 艦長預估，在超大型資料中心（Hyperscale Data Centers）中，ARM
架構CPU的市佔率在未來數年內將正式超越  50%，壓過傳統X86架構。Intel雖然開始為CSP代工伺服器處理

器，但進度落後且難以逆轉大局。
四大CSP（雲端服務供應商）自主CPU佈局：
Amazon：具備成熟度最高的Graviton系列處理器。
Google：全面推動自研Axion CPU。
Microsoft：推出自研Cobalt CPU。
Meta：與SocialNexus合作開發的自研CPU方案亦預計於今年（2026年）開始陸續開出規模。
第三方ARM處理器晶片商： 在四大CSP自研CPU之外，市場主要由NVIDIA的Grace  CPU、博通、以及高通
（Qualcomm）等業者積極搶進。ARM官方自行推出的微架構方案，由於商業模式限制，在資料中心CPU
的直接市佔率反而不及上述晶片大廠。

### Page 3

五、 電力與超高壓供電架構（800V）對機櫃設計的影響
800V 高壓供電的必然性： 隨著AI機櫃總體功耗從過去的120kW一路上升至下一代架構的200kW+，傳統
的低壓高電流供電模式會面臨嚴重的線路損耗（I²R  Loss）。因此，機櫃內部或資料中心端直接導入800V高
壓供電架構，配合HD-VPC（高密度垂直供電）已成為產業必經之路。
HD-VPC 與 800V 導入時程： 供應鏈原先預期，800V高壓系統與超高功率電力機櫃（HD-VPC）要到下一代
更遙遠的架構才會全面普及。然而根據最新調研，大客戶與NVIDIA在最新的先進機櫃設計（如B200/
Ultra、甚至是後續的Rubin平台周邊）中，已提早將800V與高壓電力架構（如54V轉高壓等方案）列為積極
導入項目。這將加速電源管理晶片（PMIC）、高壓MOSFET、及變壓元件供應鏈的產值提升。
【分析師參考】2026-2029 關鍵晶片及技術進程預估表

應鏈變動風險。

### Page 2

FIFA2026世足賽經濟效益商機可期
1資料來源：Statista、FIFA 、中信投顧整理
世足賽
百年來世界盃主辦國
• FIFA 世界盃(World Cup)由國際足球總會(FIFA)主辦，每 4 年舉辦一次，是全球規模最大、收
視率最高、影響力最深遠的單項體育賽事，其狂熱程度與吸金能力甚至超越奧運會。
• FIFA2026的參賽隊伍擴增至48隊(+50%)，總場次達104場(+62%)，將實質帶動球衣、足球鞋
及周邊裝備消耗量增長 。
• 上屆世足創造約170億美元經濟效益，本屆結合美洲主場強大消費力，相關品牌商機與營收規
模有望創歷史新高。而台股製鞋與紡織代工廠隨世足賽效益，帶動需求和拉貨力道提升。
歷年FIFA收入趨勢 (USD,B)

### Page 3

世足賽帶動旅遊、廣播、運動用品及相關消費需求
2資料來源： FIFA，中信投顧整理
世足賽
世足賽受惠行業經濟效益
• FIFA2026舉辦時間為2026/6/11/~7/19，由美加墨三國聯合舉辦，主辦城市高達16座，可望
帶動旅遊、飯店、房產、廣播、運動用品等消費經濟產業收入動能。
受惠產業 / 領域 經濟效益說明（核心亮點）
廣告和贊助 廣告商和品牌可以通過贊助世界盃賽事來提升品牌曝光度，吸引全球觀眾的注意。
體育賭博 世界盃期間體育賭博活動會大幅增加，這為賭博公司和相關機構帶來了龐大的收入。
旅遊業 世界盃吸引了大量球迷前往參加比賽，可為當地的旅遊業帶來了巨大的商機，包括住宿、餐飲、交通等。
電子商務 球迷會在賽事期間增加購買球衣、紀念品等商品，這為電子商務平台和零售商帶來了商機。
媒體和網絡平台 世界盃賽事的轉播權和網絡直播權是非常有價值的資產，媒體和網路平台可以通過播放賽事來吸引廣告收
入。
賽事觀眾收入 大量的觀眾會購買門票，這對主辦國的經濟有直接的影響。
基建城市發展 世界盃的舉辦通常需要大規模的基礎設施建設，這對城市的發展有積極的影響。

### Page 4

2026年世足賽贊助商
3資料來源： FIFA、各公司官網，中信投顧整理
世足賽
2026年FIFA主要合作夥伴及贊助商
• 2026年世足賽的贊助商體系主要分為三個級別：FIFA官方合作夥伴、世界盃官方贊助商以及賽
事支持商與供應商。麥當勞、樂事、百威英博等全球餐飲與零售龍頭依舊可見。
• 現代贊助不再只是在球場圍欄看板刷存在感，更強調數位變現與會員忠誠度計畫，各大贊助商
紛紛透過官方 APP 推出賽事預測、限定虛擬與實體周邊，甚至開放會員以點數兌換極其珍貴
的世足賽門票或頂級觀賽體驗，藉此深度綁定消費者。
第一級：FIFA 合作夥伴 第二級：世界盃官方贊助商 第三級：區域官方支持者
最頂級全球合作夥伴，享
有國際足聯旗下所有賽事
與推廣項目的獨家權益。
本屆世界盃專屬全球合作夥伴，圍
繞美加墨世界盃大賽展開全方位的
商業推廣與曝光。
專注於各大洲或賽區當地的市場營銷與本地
支持。

西隊Jordan專屬球衣，並發表全新材質足球鞋。配合全球逾

5,000家門市展示，有望全面受惠世足賽周邊買氣。
Visa (V) 支付
FIFA 官方獨家支付技術合作夥伴。跨國球迷在美、加、墨的消
費將直接貢獻手續費，賽事亦提振美國與拉美的入境旅遊支付
量，並透過與客戶合作的專屬行銷活動帶動加值服務收益。

上銀 (2049 TT)  Trading Buy

考量近期機器人題材降溫，評等調整為 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：雖然上銀部份產品 03/2026 漲價，預估提高毛利率
的效應 06/2026 開始發揮， 2026 年獲利展望佳。不過，近期機
器人題材降溫，上銀股價受系統性風險影響程度較大，不利於
短線股價表現，故群益將上銀的投資建議調整為 Trading Buy 。 
2026 年自動化產業前景可期： 上銀 05/2026 營收 24.80 億元，
MoM+0.24% ， 01~05/2026 營收 YoY+15.37% ，主要係受惠於中
國市場需求開始回溫，根據多家重要客戶表示，買氣至少可維
持至 2026 年年底，此外，上銀 03/2026 月底調高部份產品報價，
以反應原物料、電費、薪資等成本走揚，群益認為，此舉顯示市
場供需秩序轉佳。  
根據日本機器人工業會 (Japan Robot Association ， JARA) 指出，
受惠於自動化需求增溫、 AI 相關投資，  1Q26 日本工業機器人
接單數量為 6.04 萬台， YoY+17.72% ，接單金額為 2,947.99 億
日圓， YoY+40.96% ，已連續 7 季維持正成長，單季訂單接單金
額連續 2 季創歷史新高，換言之，自動化市場接單狀況已見谷
底復甦跡象， JARA 並預估， 2026 年日本工業機器人接單金額
將增加至 1.22 兆日圓 (01/2026 的預估值為 1.03 兆日圓 )，
YoY+16.7% ，將創歷史新高，換言之，自動化產業前景可期。  
AI 機器人為上銀長線潛在營收動能： 目前上銀 AI 機器人相關
訂單來源主要有三， (1) 美國物流機器人廠， (2) 美國人形機器人
整機廠， (3) 台灣人形機器人代工廠，目前以美國物流機器人相
關產品開發進度最快，預計 2026 年開始小批量出貨。此外，上
銀並開發出行星式滾柱螺桿，此一新產品係用於重負荷傳動裝
置，為電動伺服傳動系統重要零組件，由於各大機器人廠基於
環保考量而陸續以電動伺服傳動系統取代傳動油壓傳統系統，
滾柱螺桿需求隨之逐年成長。整體而言，上銀積極佈局 AI 機器
人領域，雖然 2026 年僅美國物流機器人相關產品將開始小批量
出貨，但長線發展想像空間可期，為上銀重要潛在營收動能。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
上銀因應氣候變遷、溫室氣體乃採取減緩與調適措施，並擬定策略實施短中
長期目標，依此實施篩選重大風險與機會，並依據政策與法規、市場與生產
製造等面向，分別進行氣候變遷風險分析評估潛在財務衝擊。  
上銀使用 IPCC 2007 年全球升溫潛能值，以及環保署公告之國家排放係數，
進行 ISO 14064-1 盤查作業，包括營運總部、工二廠、精科二廠、雲科一廠、
雲科二廠  、雲科三廠、大埔美一廠、大埔美三廠等廠區已陸續完成驗證，
上銀並將持續製程改善、綠色生產，並在生產過程中致力於節能減碳，符合
對客戶、環境之綠色承諾。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2404 漢唐(UIS)

報告日期： 2026/06/12  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 半導體大客戶海內外擴產持續 
漢唐為台灣半導體大廠之長期配合廠務工程公司，近年隨半導體
需求強勁，半導體廠於全球建廠，推動漢唐工程案大幅成長，國
內大客戶於新竹、台中、高雄持續建置 2nm 及 2nm 以下之先進製
程，海外方面，美國 Fab21 P2 已完工過半，預估 2026 年底認完 9
成，儘管美國廠建廠成本為台灣數倍，然漢唐已有建廠經驗，在
人力調配、成本控管上掌握度更高，在工程效率有所提升，預估
接下來美國廠建廠之毛利率有望回升。

二. 1Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 16.17 元 
漢唐 1Q26 營收 202.88 億(QoQ-1.3% 、YoY+76.15%) ，受惠於建廠需
求強勁，營收淡季不淡，毛利率提升至 23.32%優於預期，主要因
工程執行效率提升，並反應之前預提之成本，稅後淨利 30.81 億
(QoQ+16.51%、YoY+54.88%)，EPS 16.17 元。

三. 2026 年毛利率改善，預估全年 EPS 85.15 元 
展望 2026 年 ， 公司至 115 年 5 月之已簽約未認列之金額 1939 億元 ，
創歷史新高 ， 預估隨著客戶 P3 陸續下單 ， 未認列金額將持續上升 。
台灣市場 Foundry 大客戶建廠持續 ， 而美系記憶體廠亦有統包案挹
助; 海外部分，美國 P2 陸續認列營收至年底，並開始 P3 之建廠，
而美國之工案因反應成本單價較高，而在與下包商配合後，執行
效率提升，且因客戶積極推進工程進度，帶動美國建廠之毛利率
提升。預估漢唐 2026 年營收 1072.97 億(YoY+62.34%)，稅後淨利
162.28 億(YoY+78.93%)，EPS 85.15 元。預估 2027 年營收 1446.97
億(YoY+34.86%) ， 稅後淨利 227.22 億(YoY+40.01%) ， EPS 119.22 元 。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 1550 元 
投資建議方面，漢唐為專業半導體廠務工程公司，營運表現高度
連動半導體資本支出及半導體廠之新建廠計劃，受惠於 AI 需求帶
動先進製程、記憶體供不應求，半導體無塵室已成為市場緊缺資
產，在全球廠務工程供不應求下，接單價格亦優於過往，漢唐在
半導體客戶海內外擴產持續下訂單無虞。考量到公司訂單能見度
提高 、 今明年營收獲利創高 ， 參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 13 倍本益比，
目標價 1550 元，建議買進。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
漢唐成立於 1982 年 9 月，主要業務為服務高科技產業之建廠及擴
廠計劃，業務範圍包括半導體、光電、封裝測試、太陽能、LED、
生技等產業。公司為專業提供高科技產業建廠之系統集成服務公
司；主要業務範圍包括技術顧問服務、高科技整廠集成專案及電
腦、控制、無塵室、機電、電信系統之專案業務。2025 年營收比
重為系統整合佔 99.5%、維護服務佔 0.1%、設計業務/產品銷售占
0.4%。公司主要客戶包括台積電、友達、日月光、力晶、南科、
旺宏、泰林、彩晶、華邦電、南茂、精材、南京熊貓、華星光電、
深超光電、美光等工程訂單。無塵室及機電統包工程競爭者：帆
宣、同開、漢科、聖暉、擎邦、亞翔、千附。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望  
1. 半導體大客戶海內外擴產持續 
漢唐為台灣半導體大廠之長期配合廠務工程公司，近年隨半導體
需求強勁、全球供應鏈重組，帶動半導體廠於全球建廠，亦推動
漢唐工程案大幅成長，其中漢唐大客戶先進製程供不應求，近年
於國內外續擴建新廠，國內部分，大客戶於新竹、台中、高雄持
續建置 2nm及 2nm以下之先進製程 ， 將挹助漢唐訂單能見度達 2030
年; 另因大客戶至美國設廠，漢唐在一廠設置時即配合大客戶於

2020 年 11 月成立美國分公司，並於 2022~2023 年挹助營收大幅成
長，大客戶美國 AZ Fab21 P1 已開始量產，P2 目前施作中，已完
工過半，預估 2026 年底認完 9 成，客戶 P3 已小量下單、P4 規劃
中 ， 而 Fab21 為超大規模的先進製程聚落 ， 總計將建置 6 座晶圓廠 、
2 座先進封裝廠及 1 個研發中心，預期漢唐未來五年接單無虞。儘
管當初因為美國廠建廠成本為台灣數倍，加上人工、材料成本，
法規等影響使得建廠成本較原先預期為高，然漢唐已有上次建廠
經驗，在人力調配、成本控管上掌握度更高，在工程效率有所提
升，加上也會跟客戶反應高漲之成本，因此預估接下來美國廠建
廠之毛利率有望回升。

求強勁，營收淡季不淡，毛利率提升至 23.32%優於預期，主要因
工程執行效率提升，並反應之前預提之成本，稅後淨利 30.81 億
(QoQ+16.51%、YoY+54.88%)，EPS 16.17 元。

YoY+167.22%)，EPS 19.6 元。

展望 2026 年 ， 公司至 115 年 5 月之已簽約未認列之金額 1939 億元 ，
創歷史新高 ， 預估隨著客戶 P3 陸續下單 ， 未認列金額將持續上升 。
台灣市場包含新竹、台中、高雄之半導體建廠持續，而美系記憶
體廠亦有統包案挹助; 海外部分，美國 P2 陸續認列營收至年底，

股價相對大盤走勢

興富發 (2542 TT)  Buy

2026 〜 2027F 營收獲利起飛，超配現金股利吸睛。  
投資建議： 2026~2027 年將為興富發近 5 年營收獲利爆發期，
4Q25 遞延案量及潤隆 2026 年有望貢獻 320 億元營收，預期興
富發 2026 年將有 12 筆新完工案啟動交屋。 2026 年完工案亦將
持續貢獻 2027 年營運動能，預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 分別達
6.47 元 /8.04 元。股利政策方面， 2026 年預計配發每股現金股利
4 元，盈餘配發率達 190% ，以 06/10/2026 收盤價換算，現金殖
利率約 8.8% 。目前股價處於 PBR 歷史區間 1.27X 〜 2.27X 中間
值，因此對興富發首次投資建議為 Buy ，目標價 54 元 (2026 年
PBR1.75X) 。  
2026 年大手筆配發現金股利 4 元： 2026 年將配發 2025 年度每
股現金股利 4 元 ，盈餘配發率達 190%，超 額 配 發。以06/10/2026
收盤價換算，現金殖利率約 8.8% 。  
2026 年將迎來近 5 年最大完工交屋潮，預估稅後 EPS 倍增至
6.47 元：受惠部分 4Q25 完工個案，預期 1H26 仍有 5 筆中南部
遞延交屋。考量到 2026 年將有 12 筆新案完工交屋，加上先前
已完成 2025 年部分低毛利案去化，以及潤隆 4Q25 有 3 筆遞延
交屋及溫莎堡、潤隆鉑悅等 2 筆新案交屋，有望貢獻 320 億元
營收。研究部預估興富發 2026 年營收 1,002.38 億元，
YoY+222.76% ，稅後純益 140.59 億元， YoY+216.85% ，稅後 EPS 
6.47 元。若以過去約 80~100% 現金配發率中上緣為基礎，推估
2027 年有望配發 6 元現金股利，以 06/10/2026 收盤價換算，現
金殖利率達 13% 。  
2027 年多筆商辦案完工入帳，預估毛利率較 2026 年略增： 2027
年有望交屋入帳個案共 16 筆。除部分 2026 年欲推尚未公開銷
售個案外，平均銷售率約 75% ，預期交屋進程應相對順利，加
上 2027 年有多筆商辦案交屋入帳，毛利率有望較 2026 年略增，
以及潤隆約 100~110 億元營收貢獻。研究部預估興富發 2027 年
營收 1,132.57 億元， YoY+12.99% ，稅後純益 174.68 億元，
YoY+24.25% ，稅後 EPS 8.04 元。

2 群益投顧 
  興富發建設 ESG： 
興富發遵循全球永續性標準理事會 GSSB 發布之 GRI Standards 、聯合國永
續目標 SDGs 、同業標竿案例、營造及不動產行業補充指南蒐集永續議題，
結合歸納出包含公司治理、環境及社會公益等 3 大面向中 26 項永續議題。
公司治理方面， 11/14/2023 董事會通過設立永續發展委員會，並於 2024 年
度召開 3 次例行性會議；環境永續方面，以蒐集相關氣候風險資料、分析評
估、制度相應策略、定期檢視執行成效及瞄準未來方面等 5 大步驟執行風險
評估，並推出台中七期市政萬象廣場、台中市政壹號廣場等微型商辦。以智
慧建築為建築平台 ， 打造兼具建築裝置 、 自動化及通訊網路系統 ， 並成立BIM
建築資訊模型中心，降低耗能。社會公益方面，持續著墨於在地關懷及地方
創生，落實多元共榮、公益志工假。  
公司簡介： 
興富發建設成立於 01/23/1980 ，前身為鋐福建設股份有限公司，主要經營業
務包含國民住宅出租出售及委託營造業者興建商業大樓。 05/1989 因合併宏
巨建設，更名為宏巨建設股份有限公司， 2000 年再次合併興富發建設股份
有限公司，於 05/06/2003 正式更名為興富發建設。目前主要推案區域涵蓋
全台各地，包括大台北、桃園、新竹、台中、台南、高雄等都市中心。期下
建設事業有興富發建設、潤隆建設、博元建設及齊裕建設；飯店事業有 3 個
據點、 3 家營運管理合作夥伴及新北北海溫泉洲際酒店、台南安平亞樂軒飯
店、高雄凱悅酒店。  
在手開發案： 
興富發建設在手建案共 38 筆，預估合計總銷 4,957.52 億元，主要分布於中
南部。台北 3 筆，總銷 444.74 億元；新北 2 筆，總銷 607.66 億元；桃園 4
筆，總銷 390.01 億元；新竹 1 筆，總銷 147.89 億元；台中 14 筆，總銷 1,810.3
億元；台南 2 筆，總銷 149.41 億元；高雄 12 筆，總銷 1,407.51 億元。未來
仍將積極參與合建案開發，都更與危老都市更新，且以商辦住宅雙軌並行，
主要鎖定重大建設發展、優質學區及公園綠地、交通便利與機能成熟區域等
議題。

3 群益投顧 
央行再次釋出新放寬政策，房市軟著陸可期： 
自 09/01/2025 起申請新青安貸款個案，將不計入銀行法第 72 條之 2 上限
30%；換屋族出售舊屋的期限延長半年至 18 個月，且 09/20/2024( 含 )後之貸
款案件皆適用； 2026 年起銀行不動產放款總量將回歸由各銀行內部自行控
管，不再需要給出預定目標，僅須按月向央行彙報相關資料即可；自
03/20/2026 起全國自然人第 2 戶購屋貸款成數上限由 50% 上修至 60% 。  
公司表示，在 09/2025 新青安不計入銀行法 72-2 後，首購族群有感受到善
意， 10/2025 整體銷售率略增。期望政府可針對都更危老案件開放更多融資
限制。另外，公司亦表示微型商辦、商辦市場 (企業總部 )因需求、總價及區
域面等因素，將為未來發展趨勢。  
2026 年大手筆配發現金股利 4 元： 
2026 年將配發 2025 年度每股現金股利 4 元，盈餘配發率達 190% ，超額配
發，顯示公司對其未來各年度完工案量深具信心。以 06/10/2026 收盤價換
算，現金殖利率約 8.8% 。公司表示，未來股利政策仍將維持年配機制及高
盈餘配發率，並以現金股利為主。

受惠部分 4Q25 完工個案，預期 1H26 仍有 5 筆中南部遞延交屋及 3 筆新完
工案開始交屋。鉑金愛悅、雍悅一方、紀汎希、卓越城、美術 1 號院等 5 筆
將於 2~3Q/26 完工，預期多數 3Q26 初啟動交屋。 3Q26 底 ~4Q26 初亦有望
開始國家企業廣場、當代文匯、蘿曼羅蘭、森悅等認列入帳。考量到 2026
年將有 12 筆新案完工交屋，加上先前已完成 2025 年部分低毛利案去化，以
及潤隆 4Q25 有 3 筆遞延交屋及溫莎堡、潤隆鉑悅等 2 筆新案交屋，有望貢
獻 320 億 元 營 收。預 期2026 年營收 、 獲利倍數成長 。 研究部預估興富發2026
年營收 1,002.38 億元 ，YoY+222.76% ，稅 後 純 益140.59 億元 ，YoY+216.85% ，
稅後 EPS 6.47 元 。 若以過去約80~100% 現金配發率中上緣為基礎 ， 推估 2027
年有望配發 6 元現金股利，以 06/10/2026 收盤價換算，現金殖利率達 13%。
不畏房市冷淡，2026 年預計推案千億，展現多年經營韌性： 
2026 年預計推出 7 筆新案，合計總銷 1,155.91 億元。包括新北國家壹號廣
場 (總銷 295.9 億元 )、桃園 VVS1 鑽石經貿中心 (總銷 94.4 億元 )、桃園中路
28( 總銷 66.58 億元 )、台中惠國 88( 總銷 127.88 億元 )、台中惠國 90( 總銷
427.27 億元 )、高雄新興段 (總銷 84.4 億元 )、高雄博孝三 (總銷 59.48 億元 )。
2027 年多筆商辦案完工入帳，預估毛利率較 2026 年略增： 
2027 年有望交屋入帳個案共 16 筆。其中包含台北中山國家企業廣場、台北
北投當代文匯、桃園大園美術水公園、台中西屯蘿曼蘿蘭、台中西屯鉑金愛

9 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

DC Delay
On June 9, SemiAnalysis reported that Nvidia’s (covered by Joseph Moore) planned
large-scale shipments of native 800V DC power architecture have been pushed
back to 2028, later than market expectations, while +-400V remains on schedule
for hyperscaler's ASIC deployments. Nvidia has reportedly denied the reports
.
Our take: This news is contrary to our supply chain checks at Computex. As we
stated in our Computex read-through note, NVDA at GTC Taipei indicated 800 VDC

developments are ongoing with 800 VDC power rack being ready for mass
production in 3Q26. +-400 VDC development has been redirected to 800 VDC
focus across various hyperscalers. (Exhibit 1 ).
We also note Delta will likely be the first vendor to mass produce 800 VDC
standalone power racks in 4Q26 to one of major hyperscalers in the US. We
understand the initial shipment volume won't be enormous and it will take some
time for 800 VDC protection device development and industrial safety standards to

be defined by UL and major regulations. However, 800 VDC developments remains
intact, in our view.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Epiwafer price hikes
Double-digit increases for GaAs and InP from June
VPEC announced a price hike on epiwafers, with both GaAs and InP to see double-
digit increases from June. This is the second time it has raised prices this quarter,
which strengthens our confidence in a better margin recovery ahead. We still
expect substrate supply to improve in coming quarters, with sales to regain

momentum from June, followed by double-digit sequential growth in 3Q26. We
remain positive on VPEC due to share gain opportunities in EML/CW lasers into
next year and structural margin improvement, and our 2027/28 EPS forecasts are
36%/26% above consensus. Maintain Outperform with TP of NT$465.
q VPEC announces epiwafer price hike: Prices for both GaAs and InP epiwafers will
increase by double digits from June (with greater hikes for InP) to reflect higher input

costs. This is the second time VPEC has raised epiwafer prices this quarter, which
strengthens our confidence in a better margin recovery from 2Q26.
q Sales momentum to pick up from June: While QTD sales are tracking slightly behind
our forecast of +10% QoQ due to substrate shortages, we believe momentum will
pick up from June onwards, followed by double-digit QoQ growth in 3Q26, backed by
additional MOCVD capacity, better substrate supply and stronger backlog orders.

q Key beneficiary of InP shortages: VPEC is in a unique position as a third-party
epiwafer house, which allows it to benefit from the growing outsourcing trend among
global optical chip leaders, and it is likely to gain 2-3 new clients for EML/CW lasers in
early 2027. We expect VPEC to outperform the sector growth in coming years.
q Maintain Outperform: Our 2027/28 EPS forecasts are 36%/26% above consensus as
we expect stronger optical growth with better margins. Trading at 28x 27SP EPS

versus the sector average of 48x, we see better risk/reward in VPEC’s share price, and
expect the improved sales momentum from June to help it catch up. Our target price
of NT$465 is based on 40x 27SP PE and we reiterate an Outperform rating.

Valuation details
We use PE as our valuation methodology as we believe the market will primarily
focus on the earnings growth outlook, which is better reflected with PE metrics.
The stock has traded largely between -1sd (20x) and +1sd (40x) for the past seven
years (after the structural rerating since 2017 on optical and China localisation

growth) based on each phase of the industry cycle. We assign an upcycle PE
multiple of 40x to reflect a stronger optical outlook.
Investment risks
1) Slower-than-expected rollout of 1.6T PD products; 2) weaker-than-expected
smartphone sell-through; 3) consumer optical projects further delayed into 2027.
Foreign investors hold 25%

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Wiwynn (6669.TW): General servers turning consignment supporting
GM while reducing revenues; ASIC AI servers to grow ahead; Buy
12 June 2026 | 7:40PM HKT
Wiwynn’s May revenue was flat MoM, or 12% below our estimate, due to the switch
to a consignment business model for some server projects starting from Apr (Read
more). Despite the business model transition set to bring lower revenues, the GM
would be better and also ease working capital burden in the long term. For coming

June revenues, we expect a sequential increase on strong general servers demand
from leading US CSPs, followed by 23% QoQ growth in 3Q26 driven by ASIC AI
servers ramp up. We are positive on Wiwynn’s leading market position in ASIC AI
servers, and diversifying into GPU AI server racks, and riding on leading US CSP’s
strong demand across general servers and AI servers. Maintain Buy.
Earnings revision: We factor in Wiwynn’s May revenue and raise our 2026-28E net

income by 6% / 18% / 14%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E revenues are
revised up by 4% / 24% / 11%, driven by (1) higher general server shipments,
reflec
ting the stronger demand from CSPs since late 2025, (2) product mix upgrade
towards next-generation ASIC AI servers with better ASP , (3) expanding to rack-level
GPU AI server that carries high ASP . Our 2027E GM is slightly revised down, due to

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn
We are Buy rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$7,147. Our TP is based on 15.8x
2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth, 2)
slower-than-expected recovery on general server demand, 3) more severe competition
in server ODM market.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Networking on the rise
More AI card and switch upside ahead
Accton’s outlook is strengthening on improving Trainium3 visibility and broader
800G/1.6T switch project wins. With better CoWoS supply into 2H26 and Accton
securing key PCB supply, we view Trainium3 shipment ramp-up risk as
manageable. With higher content value from Trainium3 with scale-up switch and

1.6T switch upgrades, we raise our 2026/27/28 EPS forecasts by 10%/18%/20%.
Lift TP from NT$3,100 to NT$3,500 and maintain High Conviction Outperform.
Stronger Trainium3 outlook
Although key component supply for Trainium3 remains tight, improving CoWoS
supply should support a stronger shipment ramp from 2H26. We estimate total
Trainium shipments will increase from 2.2m units in 2026 to 3.0m units in 2027.
The adoption of switch trays and rising dollar content from Trainium2 to Trainium3

should further strengthen Accton’s AI card revenue. We also believe AWS/Accton
has secured sufficient PCB supply, supporting intact shipment momentum. Overall,
we forecast Accton’s AI card business to grow by 72%/44%/40% YoY in
2026/27/28.
More networking switch project wins
Robust datacentre networking demand is driving the transition from 800G to 1.6T.
1.6T transceiver module production ramped up from 2Q26, and 1.6T switch
shipments should begin from 2H26. Accton is capable of supplying both Broadcom-

and Nvidia-based 800G switches to major CSP customers, and we believe the
company is expanding wins from existing CSP and enterprise clients towards 1.6T
switch projects with NeoCloud vendors. The move to 1.6T should also lift dollar
content, particularly if liquid cooling adoption increases.
Capacity expansion to support global demand
Accton continues to expand capacity in Taiwan, Vietnam, Texas, Singapore and
Malaysia, improving regional supply diversification. Vietnam will mainly support

800G/1.6T switch manufacturing, while the US plant is expected to support Level
11 assembly with production lines deployed by end-2026. Singapore and Malaysia
should support future optical-related demand.
Earnings revisions and valuation
After raising our 2026/27/28 EPS forecasts by 10%/18%/20% to reflect stronger
Trainium3 demand and robust networking switch momentum, we lift our TP from
NT$3,100 to NT$3,500, still based on 30x 2027SP PE. Maintain HC O-PF.

Investment thesis
Content value increase in Trainium3 will mostly benefit major L6/L10 suppliers like
Accton, which is a leading supplier in the ODM-direct switch market. Switches for
datacentres are also poised to see strong momentum driven by aggressive

hyperscaler Capex outlooks with networking speeds upgrading to 400G/800G to
support AI applications. Moreover, Accton has demonstated its expertise in AI
server components, and we believe it may be able to leverage its technology and
tap into the AI/ASIC server assembly business, coupled with opportunities from
immersion cooling projects. This should lead to expanded offerings, positioning
Accton as a total solution provider for AI servers in the future.

Catalysts
(1) Increased penetration rate of 800G switches in 2026; (2) better hyperscaler
Capex outlooks; (3) AI accelerator demand, especially for AWS ASICs; (4) progress
in liquid cooling ASIC rack projects; (5) business expansion to capture more AI
server opportunities; (6) increasing demand for optical switches.
Valuation details
Our target PE multiple of 30x is above +1 standard deviation of the five-year
average, which we believe is backed by a 35% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP, reflecting

our bullish view on Accton’s leading position in the AI accelerator and direct-ODM
switch market and near-term robust demand from AWS AI accelerators ramping up.
Investment risks
There could be downside risk to sales growth and blended gross margins if: (1) the
adoption of 400/800Gbps switches is slower than we expect; (2) datacentre Capex
is lower; (3) there is weaker AI accelerator demand and slower migration; (4) rising
chip/component costs for networking switches.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
ABF Substrates – AT&S
announces capacity expansion in
Malaysia
What's new? AT&S (ATSV.VI, not covered) announced that it had signed key terms
with AMD and another major tech customer  to add AI/HPC IC substrate capacity in
Kulim, Malaysia. The  company said the €1.5-2bn investment is fully supported and
financed by long-term customer commitments, pending final execution.
Based on the terms, AT&S raised its FY26/27 guidance sharply:

• Currency-adj. rev growth to 45-55% from 30-35%;
• EBITDA margin to 32-37% from 25-29%;
• Capex €1-1.2bn from €0.4bn, with significantly positive op FCF.
Our thoughts: AT&S raising guidance for FY26/27 on the new capacity
announcement implies it  will come online by EoFY, or March 2027, as it appears
AT&S is only adding new production lines at existing buildings at its Kulim site,
instead of building a new production facility.  This i
mplies strong demand and  does

not derail our expectation of an  ABF supply deficit from 2027 onwards, implying a
positive readacross for  Unimicron, NYPCB and ZDT. We are OW on all three stocks.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn&#39;t require substrate
(e.g., CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Zhen Ding (4958.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 10%, a medium-

term growth rate of 15% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Higher-than-expected F-PCB content increase for iPhones
n Better-than-expected production yield/share allocation for SLP
n Share gains on higher margin F-PCB pieces
Risks to Downside
n Worse-than-expected iPhone sell-through

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 104: 260616_daiwa_emc

Source PDF: all report pdf/260616_daiwa_emc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-16
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our latest view on EMC.

What’s the impact: On-track spec upgrade trend. We believe the spec 
upgrade trend remains the key growth driver for CCL vendors. As per EMC 
management, above M7-grade (including M7) revenue in 4Q25 was over 
50% and would likely reach 70% by end-2026. Before 3Q25, EMC used M6 
as the broader line to distinguish AI-related (or high-end) CCL, indicating 
the ongoing spec upgrade driven by high-speed data transmission 
requirements. We believe from 2026, M7-M9 would be the mainstream 
materials, while mainstream materials would gradually migrate to M8-M10 
from 2027. Based on our assumption, CCL vendors should benefit by 
c.30% ASP hikes from every generation upgrade, which would be a key 
growth story for EMC. As in our previous note on TUC, we further lift our 
CCL ASP by c.10% from 2H26 to reflect the increasing cost from resin, 
which is the key reason why we lift our earnings forecasts by 9-26%.

CCL grade migration roadmap. In the near term, we believe there are 
several debates on the CCL migration roadmap, with Quartz glass (Q-glass) 
fibre and polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE) resin at the centre of discussion. T o 
meet the high-end CCL requirement on electrical properties, Q-glass and 
PFTE resin became key materials in 2025 given the original M8 resin (ie, 
hydrocarbon resin) took some time to migrate to M9/M10 resin (still 
hydrocarbon). We believe the M9/10 resin has gone under certification in 2026, 
which then dissipates the importance of Q-glass fibre and PTFE resin. Also, 
EMC has devoted its majority resources to test different formulas between 
advanced hydrocarbon resin/PTFE resin and fibres (including low dk2, low dk3 
glass, and Q glass). In fact, we believe the cost performance ratio for reaching 
M9/10 CCL requirements by adopting advanced hydrocarbon resins might be 
the easier way. However, considering the potential shortage of high-end glass 
fibre, we believe end customers will likely verify multiple solutions to meet end 
demand. In sum, we see EMC maintaining its lead position on advanced CCL 
with several solutions (different combinations of resin, glass fibre, and copper 
foil) and >50% market share in AI server and high-speed switches. Of note, 
EMC will also ramp up c.0.9m (c.10% capacity) of ABF CCL in 2027E, which 
could be another key ASP driver.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. We raise our 12-
month TP to TWD6,022, based on a higher 48x PER (vs. prior: 43x) on 1-
year-forward EPS due to the steeper earnings trajectory growth. Key 
downside risk: weaker-than-expected AI server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 2-4% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our higher opex ratio assumptions.

15 June 2026

Solid position to ride on CCL spec upgrades

➢ Spec upgrade roadmap debate only short-term noise 
➢ EMC to retain leading position in CCL with full range of solutions 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; lifting 12M TP to TWD6,022

Company profile 
Established in 1992 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 1996, Elite Material Co. (EMC) is a 
leading manufacturer of copper clad laminate (CCL) and prepreg (PP), and a mass lamination foundry 
for PCB players. The company is also the world's largest green (halogen-free) CCL supplier.

股價相對大盤走勢

安普新 (6743 TT)  Trading Buy

股價漲幅遠不如加權指數漲幅，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：耳機業務表現穩健，智慧手錶、手機搖桿，以及復古
遊戲機等…新產品推動營收成長，由於營收放大進一步推升毛
利率表現，預期獲利持續向上，  3 元現金股息有助提高股價下
檔保護，但因股價漲幅遠不如加權指數漲幅，投資建議調整為
Trading Buy 。  
進入傳統淡季， 1Q26 營收 10.84 億元， QoQ-22.75% ，EPS-0.18
元：1Q26，由於年終消費旺季已過，進入傳統消費性電子淡季，
電競耳機、 TWS 耳機出貨皆呈現季節性下滑的狀態，但是新產
品已陸續在季底出貨，例如：新款手機搖桿、智慧手錶以及遊
戲機皆已開始出貨，預期將推動公司後續營運成長， 1Q26 營收
10.84 億元， QoQ-22.75% ，稅後淨利 -0.28 億元， EPS-0.18 元。  
多樣新業務量產，預估 2Q26 營收 17.50 億元， QoQ+61.43% ，
EPS0.72 元：展望 2Q26 ，除了既有的電競耳機、 TWS 耳機年度
新機陸續出貨挹注營運動能之外，印度客戶的智慧手錶出貨將
進一步放大，中系與美系品牌客戶的手機搖桿新專案放量出貨，
新接獲的遊戲機訂單具有復古外型，能運行任天堂 N64 卡匣的
遊戲，遊戲機出貨包含主機與把手故單價較高，推動營收進一
步增長，預估 2Q26 營收 17.50 億元， QoQ+61.43% ，稅後淨利
1.08 億元， EPS0.72 元。  
新業務推動營運成長，預估 2026 年營收 68.63 億元，
YoY+23.88% ， EPS2.55 元： 展望 2026 年，預期 2026 年尚未出
現大量換機需求，電競耳機、 TWS 耳機由客戶推出新產品推動
小幅成長；新產品方面，印度客戶智慧手錶、美系與中系客戶
手機搖桿，以及美系客戶復古遊戲機等…新產品進入量產，成
為推動公司營運成長主要動能，預估 2026 年營收 68.63 億元，
YoY+23.88% ，稅後淨利 3.82 億元， YoY+264.56% ，EPS2.55 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 17 日

2 群益投顧 
  安普新 ESG  
持續對員工宣導節能減碳的觀念，提醒員工隨手關閉不使用的電源，將空調
設定合理的溫度、有效運用水資源、使用電子簽核減少用紙量及確實執行垃
圾分類降低垃圾量等方式，以達到節能減碳的目標。  
進入傳統淡季， 1Q26 營收 10.84 億元， QoQ-22.75% ， EPS-0.18 元：  
1Q26 ，由於年終消費旺季已過，進入傳統消費性電子淡季，電競耳機、 TWS
耳機出貨皆呈現季節性下滑的狀態，但是新產品已陸續在季底出貨，例如：
新款手機搖桿、智慧手錶以及遊戲機皆已開始出貨，預期將推動公司後續營
運成長。  
進入傳統淡季， 1Q26 營收 10.82 億元， QoQ-22.75% 。  
由於營收規模下滑，毛利率遜於 4Q25 的水準，稅後淨利 -0.28 億元， EPS-
0.18 元。  
多樣新業務量產，預估 2Q26 營收 17.50 億元， QoQ+61.43% ， EPS0.72 元：
展望 2Q26 ，除了既有的電競耳機、 TWS 耳機年度新機陸續出貨挹注營運動
能之外，印度客戶的智慧手錶出貨將進一步放大，中系與美系品牌客戶的手
機搖桿新專案放量出貨，新接獲的遊戲機訂單具有復古外型，能運行任天堂
(7974 JP)N64 卡匣的遊戲，遊戲機出貨包含主機與把手故單價較高，推動營
收進一步增長。  
多樣新業務量產，預估 2Q26 營收 17.50 億元， QoQ+61.43% 。  
雖然塑膠粒價格因美伊戰爭大漲，但受到營收規模有效放大，以及多樣毛利
率較佳的新產品加入量產，預期毛利率將優於 1Q26 ，預估稅後淨利 1.08 億
元， EPS0.72 元。  
公司宣布配發現金股利 3 元，其中盈餘配股現金 0.3 元，資本公積配股現金
2.7 元，提高股息殖利率有助於提升股價下檔保護力道。  
新業務推動營運成長，預估 2026 年營收 68.63 億元， YoY+23.88% ，EPS2.55
元：  
展望 2026 年，預期 2026 年尚未出現大量換機需求，電競耳機、 TWS 耳機
由客戶推出新產品推動小幅成長；新產品方面，印度客戶智慧手錶、美系與
中系客戶手機搖桿，以及美系客戶復古遊戲機等…新產品進入量產，成為推
動公司營運成長主要動能。  
印度客戶智慧手錶方面，印度大型消費性電子品牌廠商進軍穿戴式裝置，其
中消費級智慧手錶皆由公司代工，由於印度人口眾多，首批鋪貨出貨量大，
將能有效貢獻營收。  
手機搖桿方面，先前與美系客戶合作的第一代產品並非由公司開發，部分零
組件須由其他公司供應，以至於出貨量較少，第二代產品與客戶共同開發，
模具由公司製造，能掌握所有零組件，出貨量將顯著優於第一代；中系搖桿
品牌客戶在中國地區銷售量大，看中公司在越南製造的成本優勢，將加大手
機搖桿的訂單量。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 17 日

3 群益投顧 
美系復古遊戲機方面，該品牌商先前推出任天堂 Gameboy 掌機獲得正面迴
響，後續進一步推出能運行任天堂 N64 卡匣的復古遊戲機，由於復古遊戲
機客戶的競爭同業生產不及，加上美國市場對於高品質、能運行實體卡匣的
N64 相容現代化主機仍有強烈需求，復古遊戲機業務將能貢獻公司營收成
長。  
新業務推動營運成長，預估 2026 年營收 68.63 億元， YoY+23.88% 。  
受惠於營收規模提升，加上生產已完全移轉至越南，進一步提升成本優勢，
預期毛利率將優於 2025 年的表現 ， 預估稅後淨利3.82 億元 ，YoY+264.56% ，
EPS2.55 元。  
投資建議：  
耳機業務表現穩健，智慧手錶、手機搖桿，以及復古遊戲機等…新產品推動
營收成長，由於營收放大進一步推升毛利率表現，預期獲利持續向上，  3 元
現金股息有助提高股價下檔保護，但因股價漲幅遠不如加權指數漲幅，投資
建議調整為 Trading Buy 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

視陽 (6782 TT)  Trading Buy

矽水膠彩拋產品需求成長持續。  
投資建議： (1) 矽水膠彩拋與散光片新品在亞洲需求持續成長，
2025 年彩拋佔比達 30% ，預期 2026 年高毛利率的彩片營收佔
比將從 30% 提升至 35%~40% ， 3Q26 、 1Q27 持續擴產。 (2) 美國
網路品牌客戶 Hubble 於 1Q26 正式重啟拉貨，且歐洲市場受惠
於老花與多焦新品逐步放量， (3) 預估 2026 EPS17.04 元，投資
建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 215 元。  
1Q26 在亞洲與美洲市場雙引擎推升下，高毛利產品組合支撐
本業獲利能力維持高檔：  
1Q26 營收 12.29 億元， QoQ+15.08% ，毛利 5.63 億元，毛利率
45.81% 。營收增長主要受亞洲與美洲市場拉貨回溫驅動。毛利
率 45.82% 主因原料成本上升影響，高毛利彩片與功能性產品出
貨比重拉高。費用 2.18 億元，佔營收比重穩定維持在 18% ；業
外支出為 0.25 億元，受匯率波動等外匯因素影響。  
4 月營收 MOM-22% ， YOY-8% ，主要因為日本黃金週影響船運
堵塞，部分產品遞延至 5 月出貨，稼動率與出貨動能維持高檔。  
2Q26 起營運動能向上，產能擴張與高毛利產品組合優化：  
2Q26 能見度佳，營收將肯定高於 1Q26 。毛利率隨產出效率提
升及彩拋占比增加，仍有進步空間。  
視陽矽水膠產品單價雖比一般透明片高出兩成以上，但在中國
等市場依然具備強大議價能力與市場認可度。現在的成長邏輯，
轉向結構升級，主要來自矽水膠彩片、恆定彩片及功能型鏡片
帶動。目前彩片占比約三成，功能型產品低個位數。  
產能方面，截至 4Q25 總月產能為 4,900 萬片，包括透片 3,500
萬、彩拋 1,400 萬，其中彩拋產能呈現供不應求的滿載狀態。預
計 3Q26 擴 5,500 萬片，原定 5,200 萬因需求超前上修； 1Q27 擴
增至 6,300 萬片，目前已追加 800 萬片設備。

### Page 2

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
視陽深以企業永續經營及社會關懷為己任，致力「愛與分享」的理念貫徹，
透過公司治理，與人文教育的雙重概念結合，將 CSR 融入於公司策略，創
造企業集團的良善正面能量，並藉由企業資源與通路合作，散播到所需要的
社會各個角落。我們相信人心的美善，能讓社會更顯溫暖，透過持續性的藝
術公益及人文推廣，將企業資源廣佈任何需要的角落。  
公司簡介 ：  
視陽成立於  2004 年，主要從事隱形眼鏡之製造與銷售，產品包含拋棄式隱
形眼鏡及特殊用途隱形眼鏡，為台灣最大的矽水膠隱形眼鏡製造商。 2024 
年前三季，視陽各地區營收佔比分別為：亞洲  73% 、歐洲  21% 、美洲  7% 。
視陽近年積極拓展矽水膠隱形眼鏡市場，並與日本  SEED 公司合作推出矽
水膠隱形眼鏡產品。  
1Q26 在亞洲與美洲市場雙引擎推升下，高毛利產品組合支撐本業獲利能力
維持高檔：  
地區 1Q26營收(億) 營收佔比 YoY 
亞洲 9.73  79%  +39.4%  
歐洲 1.65  14%  -10.81%  
美洲 0.90  7%  +40.63%  
合計 12.29  100%  +29.78%  
1Q26 營收 12.29 億元， QoQ+15.08% ，毛利 5.63 億元，毛利率 45.81% ；營
業淨利 3.45 億元，營業利益率 28.07% ； EPS3.82 元。營收增長主要受亞洲
與美洲市場拉貨回溫驅動。毛利率 45.82% 較前一季的  47% 略微修正，主
因原料成本上升影響，但較去年同期的 41% 顯著提升  ，主因高毛利彩片
與功能性產品出貨比重拉高。費用 2.18 億元，佔營收比重穩定維持在 18%；
業外支出為 0.25 億元，主要受匯率波動等外匯因素影響。  
4 月營收 MOM-22% ， YOY-8% ，主要因為日本黃金週影響船運堵塞，部分
產品遞延至 5 月出貨，本業稼動率與出貨動能維持高檔。  
2Q26 起營運動能向上，主要因為產能擴張與高毛利產品組合優化：  
2Q26 能見度佳，營收將肯定高於 1Q26 。美國市場預計在去庫存完成後恢復
穩定小幅成長，毛利率隨產出效率提升及彩拋占比增加，仍有進步空間  。
費用率控制在固定比例，並持續採取多幣別自然避險策略以降低匯率波動
影響。  
視陽矽水膠產品堅持高品質與舒適度，產品單價雖比一般透明片高出兩成
以上，但在中國等市場依然具備強大議價能力與市場認可度。現在的成長邏
輯，轉向結構升級，主要來自矽水膠彩片、恆定彩片及功能型鏡片帶動。目
前彩片占比約三成，功能型產品低個位數。  
亞洲市場對彩片的需求高於歐美透明片，現有產能近滿載， 1H26 訂單已排
滿，彩片訂單能見度延伸到 2H26 。  
產能方面 ， 截至4Q25 總月產能為 4,900 萬片 ， 包括透片3,500 萬、彩 拋1,400

預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

1 
8299 群聯(Phison)

報告日期： 2026/06/12 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. AI 對存儲的需求持續強勁， 2Q26 獲利將續創新高  
群聯 05/2026 營收 228.78 億元 ，MoM+12.97% ，YoY+301.23% ，
累積前五個月營收 840.02 億元， YoY+229.02% ，主因受惠 AI 推
論對存儲系統強勁的需求， NAND 產業持續供不應求，預估 2026
～2030 年 NAND 的需求 CAGR >20% ， 但供給<20%，顯 見NAND
產業在 AI 的帶動下，已擺脫景氣循環的走勢，而且 AI 已由過去
三年的雲端訓練發展進入到地端的發展，對存儲的需求較先前更
為龐大，使供需缺口有望維持，對價格具有強力支撐， 1H26 的價
格漲勢確立，預估 2H26 仍存在漲價的條件，因此，我們預估群
聯 2Q26 營收 657.17 億元，QoQ+60.42% ，YoY+267.34% ，毛利
率再上升至 62.68% ，稅後淨利 220.89 億元， QoQ+45.56% ，
YoY+2867% ，單季稅後 ESP 101.4 元，續創新高。

二.混合型龍蝦可降低 70%的推論成本  
AI 帶動記憶體需求強勁成長，尤其進入推論 AI 後，帶動一股養龍
蝦風潮，對存儲需求大幅提升，群聯推出混合型 aiDAPTIV ，讓複
雜的任務上雲端 ， 簡單的任務走地端 ， 可為推論節省70% 的成本，
主因群聯使用 32G Cache 就可以去運行 120B 的參數資料（一般
120B 的資料要用 96G 的 VRAM 來跑） ，是因為群聯的Cache 跟
VRAM 的距離靠得很近，所以當 VRAM 要調動資料的時候，就可
以直接拿到群聯 Cache 記憶體裡面的資料 ,而且群聯的 Hybrid 解
決方案可以讓模型裡的數個專家中 ， 真正需要運作(調動資料 )的專
家獨立出來做運算，其餘沒用到的數個專家因完全不必進行資料
運算 /搜尋，就不會浪費記憶體的運作資源，使記憶體的使用效率
可以明顯提升，使群聯的 Hybrid 解決方案可以為使用者省下高達
70% 的運算成本。

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 427.54 元 
2026 年因 NAND 供不應求，將造成 Controller 的出貨量下滑，但
因價格持續上升，模組及系統的營收貢獻將大幅跳升，完全彌補
Controller 的一滑，且在 AI 強勁的需求帶動下，群聯 1Q26 的庫
存金額大幅上升至 722 億元 ， 較4Q25 增加超過一倍 ， 以因應2H26
的需求，因此，我們預估群聯 2026 年營收 2672.33 億元，
YoY+267.77% ，毛利率 61.68% ，稅後淨利 931.32 億元，
YoY+965.7% ，稅後 EPS 427.54 元。  
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：3420 元 
     8 X 2026 EPS 
參考價位：2200 元 
     5.15 X 2026 EPS 
     2026/06/11 收盤

四. 投資建議維持買進，目標價 3420 元 
群聯在景氣下行時仍持續投入 R&D ，且持續投入新產品應用的開
發，在景氣復甦時將率先受惠，此外，群聯已成功從 Consumer
廠轉型至 AI 生態系統供應商，優異的研發能力，加速新品推出的
時程，搶佔市場先機，未來高端化 /AI 產品佔營收比重將持續成
長，進一步帶動營運成長，投資建議維持買進，目標價 3420 元
(8X2026 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群聯 (8299) 成立於 2000 年，提供全球首顆單晶片 USB 快閃記憶
體隨身碟控制晶片，目前已經成為 USB 隨身碟、 SD 記憶卡、
eMMC 、UFS、PATA、SATA 與 PCIe 固態磁碟等控制晶片領域的
領導者。快閃記憶體正快速的進入消費性、工控及企業應用市場，
群聯持續在內嵌式儲存裝置 （Embedded ）、固態儲存裝置 （SSD ）
及保密性產品上進行新技術的創新。因群聯與 Nand Flash 原廠高
度配合，在記憶體供貨上較其他競爭對手具彈性，亦有利於產品
開發。

二.營運概況及展望  
1、產品應用範圍擴大有利營運成長 
PCIe 主要功能為 CPU、南北橋 I/O 等晶片的內部傳輸溝通介面，
PCIe 4.0 規範於 2017 年制定完成，速度較 PCIe 3.0 增加一倍至
16GB ，因應資料中心、伺服器儲存資料量速度的增加，及滿足
AI 、HPC 等高速運算對儲存裝置的資料傳輸速度越來越快的要
求 ， 給予群聯新的應用商機 。 群聯08/2018 開始與 AMD 洽談合作 ，
07/2019 配合 AMD 的 Ryzen 處理器及 X570 晶片組的推出 ， 群聯
正式量產相對應的 SSD 控制晶片，此產品的推出亦拓展了群聯新
市場的應用，預估未來商機將由目前的快閃記憶體控制晶片及儲
存產品 ODM 的 67 億美元，增加至 82 億美元，新增的應用包括
遊戲主機 4 億美元、電競電腦 3.7 億美元、汽車應用 1.1 億美元、
資料中心 5.2 億美元 、IP 授權 1 億 美 元。由 於AI 的發展超乎預期 ，
在 AI Server 帶動的高算力、高速傳輸需求的墆動下， PCIe 5.0
的規格亦陸續導入，群聯亦將以更先進的半導體製程節點提供客
戶高性能低功耗控制晶片，有助營運持續成長。此外，因應邊緣
AI 需求大幅成長將帶動微調需求同步增長，群聯自主開發的
aiDPTIVE 將開花結果陸續貢獻營收 ， 有望成為未來營運動要成長
動能。

### Page 3

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
2、成功轉型為技術導向的公司，高值/端化產品為未來營運主力 
群聯成立之初主要以消費型記憶體控制晶片為主，包括 USB 、通
路端 SSD 合計消費型記憶體相關營收比重高達約 80+% ～90% ，
因消費性產品技術層次相對較低，且競爭激烈，獲利難以大幅提
升。群聯在積極進行營運轉型的努力下，效益逐漸顯現， 2Q23 
Consumer Module 相關的產品營收比重 26%，而 Non-Consumer
相關的產品，包括控制晶片、 Embedded ODM Module 、Gaming 
Module 、Industrial Module 等，合計佔比已超過 70%，預估 2025
年 Non-Consumer 產品佔比將超過 80% ，為未來營運主力，但為
維持與客戶的議價能力及出海口，群聯將維持一定的比例的
Consumer 產品。此外，在技術方面，群聯亦有顯著提升，過往群
聯主要在 Consumer 市場發展，且扮演產品開發追隨者，自 2019
年起群聯已高始朝高值 /端化產品發展，在工業市場逐步展現開發
成果，且順利切入 PCIe Gen 4 SSD 控制晶片市場，陸續獲得多
家原廠訂單，領先同業競爭者，產業地位逐漸提升，和 Tier 1 客
戶接觸 Design in 機會變多 ，預估在2019 ～2025 年群聯將由市場
追隨者變成技術開發領先者， 2025 年後將成為全球最大 SSD 控
制晶片供應商。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

1、預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 101.4 元 
NAND 產業在 AI 的帶動下，已呈現結構性轉變，對價格具有強力
支撐，04/2026 已漲價 20%，帶動群聯 04/2026 營收達 202.07 億
元，MoM+10.32% ，YoY+237% ，預估 2H26 仍存在漲價的條件。
在有利的產業及價格環境的帶動下，我們預估群聯 2Q26 營收
657.17 億元， QoQ+60.42% ，YoY+267.34% ，毛利率再上升至
62.68% ，稅後淨利 220.89 億元， QoQ+45.56% ，YoY+2867% ，
單季稅後 ESP 101.4 元，續創新高。

2、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 427.54 元 
2026 年因 NAND 供不應求，將造成 Controller 的出貨量下滑，但
因價格持續上升，模組及系統的營收貢獻將大幅跳升，完全彌補
Controller 的一滑，且在 AI 強勁的需求帶動下，群聯 1Q26 的庫
存金額大幅上升至 722 億元 ， 較4Q25 增加超過一倍 ， 以因應2H26
的需求，因此，我們預估群聯 2026 年營收 2672.33 億元，
YoY+267.77% ，毛利率 61.68% ，稅後淨利 931.32 億元，
YoY+965.7% ，稅後 EPS 427.54 元。

8487 愛爾達(ELTA )

報告日期： 2026/06/11 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 2.58 元 
愛爾達 1Q26 營收金額 3.87 億元， YoY+58.4% ，QoQ+77.93% ，
營業利益 0.87 億元， YoY+155.47% ，QoQ+180.63% ，稅後盈餘
0.68 億元， YoY+155.65% ，QoQ+154.00% ，稅後 EPS 2.58 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.54 元 
2026 世界盃將在 2026/6/12 ～2026/7/19 於美國，墨西哥與加拿
大盛大舉行，往年世足賽往年參賽隊伍 32 隊， 2026 年增加至 48
隊，比賽場次也從先前 64 場增加至 104 場，大幅增加轉播場次也
間接提升愛爾達營運，預估愛爾達 2Q26 營收金額 5.22 億元，
YoY+77.9% ， QoQ+35.03% ，預估營業利益 0.86 億元，
YoY+229.67% ， QoQ-0.75% ，預估稅後盈餘 0.67 億元，
YoY+152.65% ，QoQ-1.52% ，預估稅後 EPS 2.54 元

三. WBC ，世足賽賽事轉播，帶動 2026 年營運成長  
愛爾達 2026 年 ， 在重大賽事陸續於2026 年舉辦 ， 包括3 月 WBC
世界棒球經典賽、 6~7 月世界盃足球賽與 9~10 月名古屋亞運等，
推估上述賽事將貢獻 2026 年營收金額 7~8 億 元，帶 動2026 年營
運恢復成長動能。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.00 元 
愛爾達 2026 年在在重大賽事陸續舉辦，包括 3 月 WBC 世界棒球
經典賽、 6~7 月世界盃足球賽與 9~10 月名古屋亞運等，上述重大
賽事皆己取得轉播權，將有助愛爾達 2026 年營運恢復成長態勢。
宏遠預估愛爾達 2026 年營收金額 17.00 億元， YoY+66.72% ，預
估稅後盈餘 2.65 億元，YoY+148.09% ，預估稅後 EPS 10.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 110 元 
愛爾達 2026 年在轉播 WBC 世界棒球經典賽，世足賽與名古屋亞
運，營運恢復成長。往年世足賽往年參賽隊伍 32 隊， 2026 年增
加至 48 隊，比賽場次也從先前 64 場增加至 104 場，大幅增加轉
播場次也間接提升愛爾達營運，有助優於往年世足賽營運。觀察
愛爾達 2026 年 ROE 34.9% ，PER8X ，ROE-PER 評價仍在低檔
水準，故投資建議買進，目標價 110 元。（11X 2026 年稅後 EPS ）

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
愛爾達科技股份有限公司 (8487.TW) 成立於 2000 年 3 月 28 日，
資本額 2.65 億元， 2015 年 12 月掛牌興櫃，並於 2024 年 3 月 26
日轉至證交所創新板掛牌上市，產業類為數位雲端。愛爾達原生
於數位，是一家具有科技 DNA 的新媒體，旗下自營六個 MOD 頻
道(體育 1 台、體育 2 台、體育 3 台、體育 4 台、影劇台、綜合台 )
及網路十個頻道，代理十個境外頻道。公司主要提供數位媒體影
音整合匯流服務，包含數位影音內容授權、數位影音頻道經營、
隨選視訊、媒體廣告託播、系統整合客製化開發專案、 APP 規劃
建置、虛擬及實體行銷活動策畫執行等項目。公司於 2003 年推出
體育賽事網路直播，並取得香港、韓國、大陸等地的影集版權，
2009 起再推出自製 HD 高畫質節目 ， 內容涵蓋美食 、 旅遊 、 綜藝、
理財、生活、體育等領域。公司於 2017 年底與合作夥伴共同推出
自製戲劇，除了鎖定台灣市場，並銷往海外。

圖一、愛爾達公司重要沿革

資料來源：愛爾達、宏遠投顧整理

二. 產業概況 
數位影音產業鏈以內容產製商、服務整合商與系統平台商為架
構，內容產製商涵蓋範圍包括廣播、電視、電影、音樂與獨立製
作公司，提供各式影音內容；服務整合商主要業務為節目與廣告
代理，視其經營地區之需求引進不同影音內容，經營範圍除上述
外尚包括網路視頻及 KTV 業者；系統服務業者如中華電信 MOD
則具備服務匯聚及系統平台的雙軌位置，在數位化的趨勢中有其
存在之必要性。

3 
愛爾達營運業務已逐步發展為兼具內容產製商與服務整合商的產
業角色，不僅在運動賽事的節目產製或是境內頻道商的經營管理
與境外頻道的代理，都是愛爾達當前的重點業務，另外愛爾達也
經營 OTT 平台，提供多元收視管道。數位影音內容透過愛爾達製
作整合，提供消費大眾在中華電信 MOD、Hami Video 、ELTA.TV
等平台上觀賞。

綜合來說，愛爾達業務範圍，在台灣數位內容產業位居上中下游
地位，不但具有內容產製能力，也擁有跨平台整合的專業服務，
且也是服務提供商，為台灣新媒體的創新領導者。

圖二、愛爾達身處產業上中下游

資料來源：愛爾達、宏遠投顧整理

根據資誠聯合會計師事務所發布「 2023 全球與台灣娛樂暨媒體業
展望報告」，剖析娛樂及媒體產業發展趨勢。報告提出 4 大主要發
現：

1，2022 年台灣娛樂暨媒體（ E&M ）市場營收年增 5.3% 、來到
186 億美元，與 2021 年 11.1% 的年增率相比，降溫許多；主要原
因是通膨延燒及全球經濟不確定性升溫，導致成長放緩。  
2，2022 年台灣規模最大的 E&M 產業為網際網路服務，占整體
E&M 營收 31.5% ，其中又以行動上網服務為主要成長引擎；其他
依序為電玩遊戲與電競 18.0% 、網路廣告 15.1% 、傳統電視
14.6% ，及 OTT 影音 9.1% 。這 5 大 E&M 產業合計貢獻台灣整體
E&M 營收近 90% 。 
3，報告指出， 2022 年台灣成長最快的 E&M 產業為 OTT 影音，
營收年增 51.5% 。

4，2023 年台灣娛樂暨媒體業營收預估將年增 4.1% ，未來 5 年並
將以 3.1% 的年複合成長率，成長至 2027 年的 217 億美元。未來
5 年成長最強勁的 E&M 產業包括電影 、 年複合成長率10.6%；OTT
影音 7.12% 、網路廣告 5.7% 、音樂與廣播 4.9% 、電玩遊戲與電
競 4.8% 、家外廣告 3.4% 。

圖三、台灣 2022~2027 年 OTT 影音市場規模複合成長率 7.12%

資料來源：愛爾達、宏遠投顧整理

資誠報告指出 ， 台灣的OTT 影音市場近年成長強勁 ， 營收在2022
年達到 18 億美元，與 2019 年的 6.27 億美元相比，幾乎翻了 3
倍 ， 主要受益於COVID-19 疫情期間訂戶對串流服務的需求大增。

資誠指出，未來台灣 OTT 影音市場成長態勢依然備受看好，尤其
目前收視戶往往有尋求「蒐集」多樣訂閱服務的傾向，預期將推
動台灣 OTT 影音營收以 7.1% 的年複合成長率 ， 成長至2027 年的
25 億美元規模。其中，按次付費隨選視訊（ transactional 
video-on-demand ）的市場仍偏利基，反觀訂閱制隨選視訊
（SVOD ）屆時可望貢獻台灣整體 OTT 影音營收逾 99% 。

綜合來說，在 OTT 影音複合成長率 7.7% ，愛爾達位於 OTT 大趨
勢地位，加上訂閱式營收成長幅度將優於產業成長率，且愛爾達
製作成本屬固定成本，邊際貢獻度持續增加，將有助愛爾達毛利
率與獲利進一步提升。

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
愛爾達營運模式主要視業務類別與上架平台不同，收入來源也會
有所差異，例如以數位內容包括體育賽事、影劇節目、綜合節目
與自製節目，分別在中華電信 MOD 平台、 Hami Video OTT 平台
與 ELTA TV OTT 平台播出，若以中華電信 MOD 平台為例，愛爾
達可在 MOD 以收視率及隨選佔比為基礎分潤； Hami Video OTT
平台來說 ， 將以收視佔比及用戶訂閱收入 、VOD 隨選佔比來分潤；
ELTA TV OTT 平台來說，就以用戶訂閱收入為主。

圖四、愛爾達營運模式

資料來源：愛爾達、宏遠投顧整理

愛爾達營收結構主要分七部分，分別為 MOD 分潤、 Hami Video
的分潤及訂閱、 ELTA TV 的訂閱、其他平台分潤、轉授權暨代購、
廣告、其他七項來源。其中以 MOD 分潤、 Hami Video 的分潤及
訂閱、 ELTA TV 的訂閱與轉授權暨代購四部分為主要核心業務來
源，另外廣告與其他業務雖然目前比重不高，但在上述業務營收
持續增加下，未來獲利邊際貢獻度將持續增加。

預估 2026 年 MOD 分潤營收金額 4.80 億元， YoY+12.1%

MOD 分潤主要指中華電信 MOD 頻道為主。愛爾達 2025 年取得
美國職棒大聯盟台灣區總代理，有機會推升 MOD 訂閱數成長，但
受到 2024 年奧運墊高營運基期， MOD 分潤 2025 營收金額 4.28
億元， YoY-0.4% 。

預期 2026 年，在重大賽事陸續於 2026 年舉辦，包括 3 月 WBC
世界棒球經典賽、 6~7 月世界盃足球賽與 9~10 月名古屋亞運等，
將有助推升 MOD 訂閱數成長，預估 MOD 分潤 2026 年營收金額
4.80 億元， YoY+12.1% 。

預估 2026 年 Hami Video 營收金額 1.6 億元， YoY+6.7%

Hami Video 主要以中華電信推出的手機 APP 應用程式為主 ， 內容
包括 MLB 、CPBL TV 、VR 館、影劇館 +、電視館、運動館等。其
中愛爾達提供中華電信 Hami Video 電視館及運動館節目內容。

愛爾達 2025 年取得美國職棒大聯盟台灣區總代理，有機會推升
Hami Video 訂閱數成長，但受到 2024 年奧運墊高營運基期，
Hami Video 2025 營收金額 1.50 億元， YoY+4.2% 。

預期 2026 年，在重大賽事陸續於 2026 年舉辦，包括 3 月 WBC
世界棒球經典賽、 6~7 月世界盃足球賽與 9~10 月名古屋亞運等，
愛爾達 2026 年 Hami Video 營收金額 1.60 億元， YoY+6.7% 。

預估 2026 年 ELTA TV 營收金額 1.15 億元， YoY+4.5%

ELTA TV，主要為愛爾達自行推出的 OTT 服務。

愛爾達 2025 年在無重大賽事舉辧，導致會員訂閱數將減少，但在
單價提升帶動估愛爾達 2025 年 ELTA TV 營收金額 1.10 億元，
YoY+19.0% 。

預期 2026 年，愛爾達 2026 年在主要重大賽事舉辦，將有助會員
訂閱數持續成長，加上愛爾達己調整新會員會費，預期 2026 年開
始顯現，預估愛爾達 2026 年 ELTA TV 營收金額 1.15 億元，
YoY+4.5% 。

預估 2026 年轉授權暨代購營收金額 7.60 億元， YoY+230.4%

轉授權暨代購，其中轉授權為愛爾達取得國際重大體育賽事後，
授權予國內其他業者轉播，代購為愛爾達替客戶取得國外影劇或
知名節目之業務。

愛爾達 2025 年取得美國職棒大聯盟台灣區總代理 ， 將有機會授權
給國內其他收視平台業者，己減緩 2024 年奧運減少的情況，愛爾
達 2025 年轉授權與代購業務營收金額 2.30 億元， YoY-65.1% 。

## Document 236: Global Semiconductors Global Semis： The CPU Renaissance？ Beneficiaries of a $223bn TAM... Bernstein 20260617

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global Semiconductors Global Semis： The CPU Renaissance？ Beneficiaries of a $223bn TAM... Bernstein 20260617.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-17
Pages: 50
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Francis Ma
+852 2123 2626
francis.ma@bernsteinsg.com
The shift from gen AI paradigm from 1.0 (chatbot) to 2.0 (agent) greatly increases
server CPU demand. As discussed in SoftBank, Arm: From GenAI to Agentic AI; Initiating
with Outperform Ratings, Agentic AI involves heavily autonomous task orchestration and
execution, which boosts the CPU workload vs. GPU. With the shift from chatbot to agentic
AI, the CPU:GPU ratio for AI data center is surging from 1:4 or 1:8 to 1:1 or higher.

We raise server CPU TAM to $223bn ($137bn in 2030 in the base case, 6x of the
2025 TAM of $37bn. This assumes $3.5tn of AI data center capex, and 1:1 CPU:GPU
pairing ratio for inference. An alternative approach with 120mn CPU cores/GW yields
similar TAM. Our previous forecast of $137bn is now the bear case (assuming $3tn AI
capex, 1:2 CPU:GPU), while the upside now sits at $330bn ($4tn AI capex, 1.5:1 CPU:GPU).
Raising Arm PT to $500, as Arm is the structural beneficiary of the renaissance

of CPUs for agentic AI. Arm architecture is suitable for agentic AI workload given its
unparalleled power efficiency. In addition, Arm is shifting from just IP provider to CPU
maker, aiming to capture $15bn revenue by CY2030, but we now forecast $22bn as we
revise CPU TAM to $223bn in 2030 (from $137bn). Arm’s 2030 EPS (FYE31) is now lifted
to $11.79 ($9.83 prior). Based on 42x P/E (40x prior), we lift Arm’s PT to $500 (21%
upside). Given the lifted PT of Arm, we also raise SoftBank PT to ¥11,200 (58% upside),

based on 30% discount to pro-forma NAV of $572bn.
Updating numbers, raising AMD and INTC PTs. Both companies should benefit from
stronger (and more sustained) server demand, though AMD’s products remain superior
for now (and we believe they will continue their share gain trajectory). Our existing AMD
model was already consistent with a stronger server CPU environment and estimates move
marginally, however we are now bringing our INTC model inline with those assumptions

and are raising estimates more materially; we also roll valuation horizon forward for both
to CY27/28 avg (vs CY27 prior) given we are about halfway through the year. Our AMD PT
moves to $600; INTC to $100. We rate AMD OP, INTC MP.
Hygon will benefit from strong x86 CPU demand and gain share in China. We expect
China to outpace global x86 growth from 2028 onward, with the easing of advanced-node
supply constraints in China and accelerating AI investment unlocking CPU potential. We

expect Hygon to steadily expand its share of China’s x86 server CPU market, exceeding
35% by 2030, as it increasingly penetrates into CSPs, beyond its traditional customer base
of government and SOEs, supported by improved interoperability with domestic AI chips
and potentially constrained supply from global vendors. We updated Hygon projection to
reflect that, revising up 2027/2028 EPS to CNY 3.6 / 6.3, raising PT to CNY 450.
What could go wrong? We’re still assessing if foundry/memory capacity will be sufficient

www.bernsteinresearch.com

INVESTMENT IMPLICATIONS
We rate SoftBank (PT=¥11,200) and Arm (PT=$500.00) Outperform.
AMD (Outperform, $600.00): Expectations remain high, but exposure to AI demand driving both a CPU and GPU story can

provide substantial growth.
INTC (Market-Perform, $100.00): Server strength is helping the company get back on their feet, and narrative/headlines may
fuel the vibe for now.

(+32% vs Bern. Old) and 71x P/E (Previously was Outperform, 80x P/E based on 2027E EPS CNY 3.48, PT CNY 280).

DETAILS
We raise our 2030 server CPU TAM to $223bn on the back of higher AI investments and strong CPU:GPU pairing ratio, and our
prior forecast of $137bn is now moved to the bear case (Exhibit 1). On the back of that, we raise PTs for Arm, SoftBank, AMD,
Intel, Hygon. Our Server CPU Industry model can be downloaded here: Server CPU Industry Model. Updated Arm financial model

can be downloaded here: Arm (Arm.US).

RENAISSANCE OF CPU IN THE AGENTIC AI PARADIGM
Since the rise of LLM, GPU/ASIC accelerators have been the core of AI computing. While training clusters once required a dense
4:1 ratio to handle heavy data-loading, the focus shifted toward eliminating the 'CPU tax' that plagued high-scale inferencing. In
custom inference-optimized deployments like Google’s TPU v6e and Meta’s Grand T eton, the GPU-to-CPU socket ratio moved
to 8:1.
Agentic AI is pushing the CPU back to center stage (Exhibit 2) because AI systems are no longer just running a model once and

returning an answer. The GPU still performs the dens maths, but the CPU increasingly determines whether the system as a
whole can orchestrate the surrounding workflow efficiently — feeding data, scheduling tasks, coordinating tool calls, manage
memory and avoid accelerator idling.
This is why the next generation of AI infrastructure is likely to see more balance in terms of hardware pairing, meaning CPU is no
longer a small support component attached to a large pool of accelerators in the agentic era. We expect the GPU-to-CPU ratio

potentially narrowing back to 1:1 from a very GPU-heavy 4:1 or 8:1 configurations. The 2026 hardware roadmaps are already
moving in that direction:
• AMD Venice: 1 CPU to 4 MI455X GPUs per compute tray.
• NVIDIA Vera: 1 CPU to 2 Rubin GPUs (4 GPU dies) per superchip.
• Google TPU7x: 1 CPU to 4 TPU chips per scale-up unit.
The GPU/CPU pairing is especially important in agentic workloads because inference is turning into a loop instead of a single
pass. A request may trigger retrieval, planning, tool use, intermediate reasoning, another model call, and then action, which

means the GPU does the heavy compute while the CPU keeps the workflow moving efficiently; if the CPU is weak, expensive
GPUs can sit underutilized, and the overall system becomes slower and less efficient.
Agentic AI also increases pressure on networking and distributed infrastructure, which strengthens the CPU’s role even further
(Exhibit 3, Exhibit 4). As workloads stretch across servers, clusters, and locations, the system has to move state, manage traffic,

and coordinate resources in real time, so the CPU becomes critical not only inside the server but across the wider data-center
fabric that supports autonomous AI execution.
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
3

### Page 4

David Dai, CFA    +852 2918 5704  david.dai@bernsteinsg.com 17 June 2026
Arm CPUs stand out in this environment because the new bottleneck is not only peak performance but efficient orchestration
under power and space limits. As operators need more CPU capacity to support growing numbers of AI agents, Arm’s pitch
around performance per watt, high core density, and scalable data-center compute becomes more compelling, which is why
agentic AI is helping bring CPUs back into focus and giving Arm a stronger strategic role in the next phase of AI infrastructure.

EXHIBIT 2: Arm argues that agentic AI shifts more work back to the CPU: accelerators generate tokens, but
CPUs orchestrate the agents, memory and workflows needed to deliver answers, making Arm’s efficient CPU
architecture increasingly critical in AI data centers

David Dai, CFA    +852 2918 5704  david.dai@bernsteinsg.com 17 June 2026
RAISING 2030 SERVER CPU TAM FORECAST TO $223BN
Within our initiation of Arm, we forecast the CY30 data center CPU TAM of $137bn, well above both Arm’s own estimates of
$100bn and AMD’s $120bn, on the basis that agentic workloads require much more CPU intensity.
Since our initiation, there are quite a few incremental developments, including better-than-expected agentic AI adoption, and

higher-than-expected AI GW and capex spending. Nvidia also guided for a $20bn revenue from Vera CPU which should also be
positive for Arm. The other notable data point that was provided by Nvidia was the annual AI infrastructure spending forecasts of
over $1tn by 2027 and $3-4tn towards the end of the decade.
Based on these data points, we revise our top-down estimates for the server CPU TAM to $223bn (Exhibit 7), with the following
assumptions:
• AI capex of $3.5 tn in 2030, implying 70 GW AI data center deployment at $50bn / GW

• AI GPU (including ASIC accelerator) market of $1.6 tn, or 45% of AI DC capex
• CPU:GPU unit ratio of 1:1 for inferencing and 0.5:1 for training, and CPU ASP as 13% of GPU
• This results in server CPU TAM of $223bn, including $174bn for agentic AI workload and $49bn for non-AI workload
We also did a sanity check based on an alternative method of CPU core count:
• According to Arm, agentic AI workload requires 120mn CPU cores / GW, vs. traditional data center requiring only 30mn

cores / GW
• This translates into 8.4bn server CPU cores for 70 GW AI data center deployment in 2030
• AI CPU TAM would be $168bn in 2030 assuming $20/core (likely conservative), similar to the $174bn calculated above
Our sensitivity analyses suggest that even a rather conservative take of this figure implies a meaningful agentic CPU
opportunity.
• In the bear case, the main assumptions are $3tn AI capex, combined with a CPU-to-GPU ratio of 0.5x for inference. The

server CPU TAM then would be $137bn, matching our previous forecast which now looks somewhat conservative.
• In the bull case, we assume $4tn AI capex, combined with a CPU-to-GPU ratio of 1.5x for inference. The resulting server CPU
TAM would be $330bn.
A sensitivity table for various assumptions is also listed in (Exhibit 8). Investors can play with these assumptions in the updated
CPU TAM model.

ARM: STRUCTURAL BENEFICIARY OF SERVER CPU TAM
ARM’S SHARE GAIN IN SERVER CPU
Why Arm could gain share from x86 in server CPU?
The core economic case is performance per watt and total cost of ownership. AWS states Graviton delivers up to 40% better
price performance versus comparable x86 instances, up to 20% lower cost, and up to 60% lower energy consumption for
equivalent workloads on certain use cases. Microsoft Azure notes that each vCPU on Cobalt 100 maps to a full physical core,

making performance more predictable for server workloads versus x86 designs that use hyperthreading to inflate vCPU counts.
Beyond economics, hyperscalers gain architectural control. Designing in-house CPUs lets them shape the memory subsystem,
I/O, accelerator interconnects, and security offload to fit their own infrastructure rather than relying on the roadmap of a
merchant silicon vendor. This is why each major CSP has now committed to a multi-generation internal CPU program built on

Arm Neoverse cores.
Above all, power consumption is one of the biggest reasons that Arm CPUs are much more likely to be adopted in AI data
centers. The world is facing an energy issue that not enough electricity is available to power the new data centers. According to
our forecast, AI accelerator power will almost 3x to reach 37 GW to be deployed in 2028. Including the power for CPU, memory,
networking, etc, the total power consumption of AI data center would surpass 50 GW. As we can’t do much to cut down power

consumption of GPU/AI accelerators, using Arm server to reduce power consumption becomes the only viable choice.

CSP CPU Roadmaps and Arm Penetration
AWS, Microsoft, and Google have each built Arm-based CPU programs (Exhibit 11) that are deepening Arm's data center

presence across generations. According to Arm, their penetration at data centers has surpassed 50% among top hyperscalers
with over 1bn Neoverse cores deployed.
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
8

### Page 9

David Dai, CFA    +852 2918 5704  david.dai@bernsteinsg.com 17 June 2026
• AWS: As the largest IaaS provider with 47% market share as of 2024, AWS is also the dominant deployer of Arm-based
instances, with 57.2% of Arm instances across the public cloud as of 2024 year-end being deployed at AWS. AWS was the
first mover to deploy Arm processors on public cloud, debuting their first arm-based instance back in 2018. As of December
24, according to Liftr Insights, Arm-based Graviton represented 25% of the total instances deployed at AWS.

• Microsoft Azure: As the second largest IaaS provider with 17% market share as of 2024, Azure also heavily deploys Arm-
based instances, with an estimated 33% of chips being Arm-based as of Q4 2024. This is a rapid ramp considering their first
Arm processor Cobalt 100 debuted only in 2024. (What performance and efficiency does Microsoft Azure Cobalt 100 VMs
deliver using Arm Neoverse performance? - Arm Newsroom)
• Google GCP: Google was the third biggest IaaS provider as of 2024 with 7% market share, and they too have ramped Arm

instances rapidly, with an estimated 21% share of all instances being Axion, which was introduced in 2024.
Why Nvidia uses Arm CPUs
Nvidia's motivation for Arm is distinct from the hyperscalers: in AI and HPC systems, the critical bottleneck is more in data
movement between the CPU, memory pool, and GPU - rather than raw CPU instruction throughput or enterprise application
compatibility. Nvidia says its Grace CPU (72 Neoverse V2 cores per die, 144 cores in the Grace CPU Superchip using dual-

die NVLink-C2C delivering up to 1.0 TB/s of LPDDR5X memory bandwidth) benefits from the NVLink-C2C interconnect that
carries up to 900 GB/s bidirectional bandwidth between the CPU and Blackwell GPU - versus PCIe Gen 5's ~128 GB/s. The
second-generation NVLink-C2C used in GB200 racks achieves 1.8 TB/s of bidirectional bandwidth, 7x faster than PCIe Gen
6. (NVIDIA Grace CPU Delivers World-Class Data Center Performance and Breakthrough Energy Efficiency | NVIDIA T echnical

Blog, NVIDIA Grace CPU and Arm Architecture | NVIDIA)
Grace was first deployed in the GH200 Grace-Hopper Superchip for HPC/AI clusters starting in 2023. The major inflection
came in 2025 when Nvidia standardized on the DGX GB200 rack form factor - bundling one Grace CPU with two Blackwell
GPUs per Superchip, with each DGX GB200 rack containing 36 Grace CPUs and 72 Blackwell GPUs, totaling 2,592 Arm
Neoverse V2 cores per rack. This shift from the standalone HGX/MGX GPU tray format toward integrated DGX rack-scale

deployment materially increased Arm CPU unit volume in AI server shipments.
The Nvidia Vera CPU (successor to Grace, launching with Rubin GPUs in 2026) further raises the bar: Vera delivers up to 1.2
TB/s of memory bandwidth — twice the bandwidth at half the power compared to traditional x86 CPUs - and is built on Nvidia's
proprietary Olympus CPU cores, a custom Arm architecture designed for Reinforcement Learning and agentic AI workloads.
Vera enables software environments to run up to 50% faster with twice the efficiency of conventional CPU infrastructure. (Next

Gen Data Center CPU | NVIDIA Vera CPU)

### Page 10

ARM AGI CPU: DRASTIC CHANGE IN BUSINESS MODEL TO CAPTURE THE GROWING OPPORTUNITY
At Arm Everywhere event in March 2026, Arm announced an important strategic move to provide their own silicon, the Arm
AGI CPU, after decades of monetizing primarily through IP licensing and royalties, making clear that the silicon offering will
complement - rather than replace - its existing IP and CSS businesses.
Their rationale was that not every client can design CPUs of their own, so companies like Meta, - which Arm identified as the

first customer, lead partner and co-developer - and OpenAI can just buy Arm’s CPUs, while other CSPs such as Amazon, Google
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
10

### Page 11

David Dai, CFA    +852 2918 5704  david.dai@bernsteinsg.com 17 June 2026
and Microsoft who can keep licensing Arm IPs for their CPU endeavours. Alongside Meta and OpenAI, they also confirmed many
other partners including Cerebras, Cloudflare, F5, Positron, Rebellions, SAP and SK T elecom. These customers are expected
to deploy Arm AGI CPU for agentic usage. Arm partners with OEMs and ODMs, as well as other supply chain partners across
cloud, memory, networking, manufacturing and many other aspects, such as AWS, Broadcom, Google, Marvell, Micron, Nvidia,

Samsung, SK hynix and TSMC among many others.
Financially, the model change is clearly margin dilutive in percentage terms, but very likely accretive in gross profit dollars
and operating profit dollars. Management’s event target was for the AGI CPU business to reach $15bn of revenue by FY31,
with total company revenue of $25bn and non-GAAP EPS of $9, with AGI CPU OPM is expected to exceed 30% by FY31. We
believe the key point is that Arm is choosing to trade some margin rate for a dramatically larger profit pool. Just as importantly,

management said the required engineering investment is already largely embedded in today’s cost base, meaning the near-term
P&L is already carrying much of the R&D burden, while the revenue opportunity from silicon only starts to appear from much
later on to bring the operating leverage once volume scales.
Our estimate bases on a more aggressive assumption and we forecast $22bn chip value as of FY31 (Exhibit 14) which roughly
equates to c. 4.9mn CPU units based on $4.6k ASP in FY31. We believe Arm silicon should generate $7.7bn OP (or 52% of

corporate OP) in FY31 in our forecast (Exhibit 15).
How we frame the server CPU model for Arm
Our key assumptions on how we think of the server CPU demand are as follows. First of all, we assume that the big 3 CSPs
(AWS / Google Cloud / Azure) continue to mainly use their own captive ASIC CPU chips.
Also, for Nvidia, we believe that aside from the CPUs bundled within the superchips (CPUs packaged with GPUs), Nvidia is to
release standalone CPU racks for agentic workloads, but the important distinction being these will be entirely sold in rack-scale

and as a system, rather than bare-metal CPUs.
Lastly, Arm AGI CPU will address the need for merchant CPU for any CSPs and/or customers who elect to build their own
systems based on Arm architectured CPUs, instead of buying a full system from a third-party vendor (including Nvidia), to suit
their specific computing needs.

Our Arm server CPU model is built bottom-up across four distinct sets of demand: 1. Nvidia-related Arm CPUs, 2. hyperscaler
in-house CPUs, 3. Arm’s own CPU silicon business, and 4. other merchant / enterprise Arm CPUs . We believe this segmentation
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
11

### Page 12

David Dai, CFA    +852 2918 5704  david.dai@bernsteinsg.com 17 June 2026
enables us to identify the different adoption driver, volume logic, royalty profile as well as strategic implications for Arm. At a
high level, we estimate the server CPU TAM rising sharply towards the end of the decade as agentic demand shifts the balance
between accelerators and CPUs. Our bottom-up 2030 server CPU TAM is $223bn, of which we estimate Arm to take $123bn
(Exhibit 16).

In terms of the different demands, firstly on Nvidia, we break it down further into GPU-attached racks and standalone CPU
racks. The former is based largely on our CoWoS-based GPU estimates and the attach rate (or rather, how much GPU is sold in
rack-scale instead of standalone), and the latter is based on how much agentic demand will be met by Nvidia CPUs, and how
many CPUs are to be attached as a result. We model it to grow significantly from 1.2mn units in CY25 to 6.1mn units in CY30

For hyperscaler in-house CPUs, such as AWS Graviton and Google Axion, we model it as a function of server unit assumptions as
well as ODM-direct server exposures, and applying Arm penetration within the CSP server base. In our model, we assume Arm
penetration to grow from 25% in CY25 to 36% in CY30 (Exhibit 18).
Including other / merchant Arm CPUs including Arm’s own AGI CPU, our bottom up TAM estimates for 2030 closely matches
the top-down $123bn, of which in Nvidia related demand comprises $58bn, CSP ASIC portion $39bn, Arm AGI CPU $22bn

(Exhibit 19), with the majority still being Nvidia / CSP-related custom chip demand.

### Page 13

ARM’S ROYAL TY IN SERVER CPUS
Royalty increase in Arm server CPUs driven by core count and increased royalty rate
Even before the AGI CPU contribution, Arm’s cloud CPU royalty model is already becoming materially more attractive. Arm said
in its Q3 2026 earnings that cloud AI / general-purpose data-center royalty revenue grew more than 100% YoY, Neoverse
deployments surpassed 1bn cores, and Arm’s share among top hyperscalers was expected to approach 50%.

The first driver is that core counts are rising fast (Exhibit 20). AWS Graviton 5 now has 192 cores, up 2x versus Graviton 4;
NVIDIA Vera has 88 Arm-based cores versus 72 for Grace; and Microsoft’s Cobalt 200 has 132 cores. At Arm Everywhere
event, management indicated that the next wave of cloud CPUs could move into the 200-300 core range and, in some cases,
toward 500 cores over time.
We think the underlying reason is the same one driving the AGI CPU opportunity: the workload mix is changing. Agentic and

always-on inference shifts more of the bottleneck toward orchestration, memory handling and coordination work, which in turn
increases the need for higher core count, power-efficient CPUs in AI clusters. Arm themselves said the shift toward agent-based
inference is redefining AI data-center designs and raising demand for CPU chips with even more power-efficient cores.
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
13

### Page 14

The second driver is higher royalty per core. Currently, Arm’s licensees pay roughly $0.50/core for first-generation v9 server
CPUs, around $1/core for first-generation CSS (Compute Subsystem), and potentially $1.50/core for later CSS generations,
with more upside in future generations. In other words, Arm is not only getting paid on more cores, but on more valuable cores,
and CSS is central to that step-up.
Arm describes CSS as pre-integrated, pre-validated, performance-validated subsystem products that help partners reach

production silicon faster. In other words, CSS sits above the core level: it wraps CPU cores with coherent interconnect, MMU/
NOC system IP, memory channels, and I/O so a chip company does not have to assemble the whole server platform from
scratch.
The company has said CSS adoption is a major tailwind to royalty growth, and customers can save substantial engineering effort
and accelerate time-to-market by up to roughly a year. Arm also says CSS customers have reported getting from kickoff to

working silicon in 13 months and saving 80 engineering years by offloading non-differentiated design and validation work. That
higher value proposition is what allows Arm to charge materially higher royalties than for core IP alone.
CSS includes more than Neoverse cores alone, bundling the cores with CMN mesh, system IP, system management, power
management, software, and development tools needed to bring a platform to market quickly. Arm positions this as the fastest

path to production silicon for cloud, AI, 5G, and networking chips because partners can focus on customization instead of
rebuilding the common infrastructure around the cores.
The cadence of product refreshes also matters. Management’s notes suggest server CPUs used to refresh every two to three
years, but increasingly move on an annual cadence, which in practice makes CSS more valuable because customers need faster
turn times to stay on the latest node and architecture. That dynamic should support both more frequent licensing activity and a

faster mix shift toward higher-royalty generations.
In cloud specifically, the early evidence of that shift is already visible. Microsoft’s Cobalt family is explicitly built on Neoverse
CSS. Company also mentioned that CSS should exceed 10% of cloud AI royalty by FY27, and rise above 30% by FY29 and
exceed 50% by FY31. If that progression is broadly right, the royalty mix should keep skewing toward higher-value server
designs over the next several years.

In conclusion, we see strong growth in Arm’s server royalty revenue (Exhibit 22), reaching $1.8bn in FY31 from ~$135mn in
FY25, driven by the increasing core count as well as higher per-core royalty rate, resulting in as structural server CPU royalty
growth even before giving Arm any credit for its own AGI CPU silicon revenue.
GLOBAL SEMICONDUCTORS
14

### Page 15

FINANCIALS AND VALUATION CHANGE
Exhibit 23 summarizes our estimate changes. We keep our estimates for all the segments other than server CPUs.
In terms of valuation, Arm has rerated significantly over the past month, and Arm’s current share price now implies 174x multiple
on 1-year forward P/E, which is near +5σ of historical trading range (Exhibit 24). As such, we believe it to be more sensible
to value Arm on FY31E financials, when the Arm AGI CPU business is expected to normalize to some degree. CY2030 is also

where most of the discussion on CPU TAM anchors on including Arm’s TAM estimates as well as our own.
Therefore, we shift the valuation anchor from our usual Q5-Q8 EPS framework to FY31E EPS, as the market debate is
increasingly centred on the company’s FY31 opportunities. We value the IP businesses at 70x and silicon businesses at
40x (30x prior, raised in expectation of acceleration in CPU market growth) respectively, blend the multiples by net income

contribution to derive a 54.5x FY31E P/E, and discount that back 2.75 years at a 10% WACC to reconcile the anchor year with
our usual 24-month forward valuation horizon.
Put simply, we use the equivalent multiple of 42x on FY31 EPS of $11.86 to derive the PT of $500 (Exhibit 28) and
reiterate Outperform.
Within this valuation framework, we believe two of the biggest swing factor for Arm would be how much the total AI capex
spending would be, as well as how much market share Arm silicon is able to take. Our sensitivity analyses (Exhibit 26, Exhibit 27)

suggest an FY31E EPS range of $9-$16, and a valuation range of $379-$677.

X86 WILL ALSO ENJOY THE TAILWIND FROM AGENTIC AI SERVER CPU WORKLOAD
Intel and AMD are currently benefitting from incredibly strong server demand, a function of both cyclical recovery following
several years of post-COVID shortfalls as well as (more recent) a sharp upward revision in demand related to agentic AI
requirements (Exhibit 31) with both Intel and AMD benefitting.
We note the company’s response (or even awareness) of the potential for agentic AI to drive CPU demand differed. Intel

appeared caught significantly off-guard (they were in fact selling off supposedly “excess” tooling last July on the belief that
demand would remain subdued, and were surprised at the end of last year where they had to scramble (suffering shortfalls in
Q1 though they are in the process of correcting their mistake now). AMD was more proactive in securing capacity, and their
sequential x86 share gains accelerated in Q1 as Intel found themselves constrained (Exhibit 32). However, Intel also benefited

from the demand environment, that was so strong in fact that they managed to sell previously-written-off parts they had
(presumably) trashed, but which still found homes as customers proved willing to buy up anything they could get their hands on.
At their analyst day in November AMD laid out expectations for a ~$60B CPU TAM by 2030 on the rise of agentic AI. Clearly the
pace and trajectory of the ramp surprised them as well though as they doubled the number in May on their Q126 earnings call

to $120B. And the company is also benefitting not only from the demand environment but also their competitive positioning as
they continue to take share given their superior product portfolio (Intel hopes to close the gap in a few years on the launch of
Coral Rapids, but admits that their current offerings leave something to be desired).
We update numbers and price targets for AMD and Intel today. Both companies should benefit from stronger (and more

sustained) server demand, though AMD’s products remain superior for now (and we believe they will continue their share gain
trajectory). Changes to our estimates can be found in Exhibit 33 (AMD) and Exhibit 34 (Intel). Our existing AMD model was
already consistent with a stronger server CPU environment and estimates move marginally, however we are now bringing our
INTC model inline with those assumptions and are raising estimates more materially; we also roll valuation horizon forward for

both to CY27/28 avg (vs CY27 prior) given we are about halfway through the year. Our AMD PT moves to $600; INTC to $100.
We rate AMD OP, INTC MP.
EXHIBIT 31: Agentic AI has driven a sharp upward revision in x86 server CPU growth this year
Source: Mercury Research, Bernstein estimates and analysis
